C:\Users\jmcdonald.NWDOMAIN\Dropbox (Town of New Windsor)\NW_Public_Sharing\Blooming Grove\2018 BG Adopted Budget 11142017.xlsx

2018 Town of Blooming Grove
Budget Comments
Budget Highlights
A Fund Revenue
o 2018 Mortgage Tax increased $65,000 due to past two years of actuals.
o Central Garage revenue was reduced by $50,000 due to anticipated changes in
the level of support provided by the Central Garage to taxing entities within the
o The 2018 senior grant from Orange County Office of the aging for congregate
meals was reduced by $23,000, from $53,000 to $30,000.
o Added $80,000 to Buildings Repair & Maintenance to repave the upper and
lower parking lot.
A Fund Appropriations
o Salary changes:
- The Town Supervisor's position was increased to $70,000 to ensure that
this full time position is the highest paid person in the town.
o Parks we added a replacement truck and the cost is included in the $42,000
that is in the equipment line.
o Engineering Contractual added $40,000 to support development of Bid
Documents for HVAC system change.
o Records management added $18,500 to the existing $1,500 to develop a
strategy for the town to move from paper to digital storage.
o Building repair and maintenance, we added $80,000 to repave the Town Hall
parking lot.
o Added $18,000 for emergency management communications devices.
Assigned to Town Supervisor, Town Emergency Manager and Town
Superintendent of Highways/DPW. $2,500 of this cost will be supported via a
possible grant. Chief of Police will have their units provided by the county.
o Added $14,000 to cover the 20% town's share for new Dial-A-Bus.
o Increased the glasses reimbursement to be $500 for all Non-union personnel.
B Fund Revenue
o Increased sales tax revenue by $30,000 due to past history.
B Fund Appropriations
o Increased Comprehensive plan funding by $10,000, total $35,000.
o Increased MS4 Funding by $5,000, total $30,000.
o Police contract will be settled for 2015 thru 2016 and impact on the B Fund has
not been assessed as of this date.
o Added salary for 2 part-time police officers.
o Increased the glasses reimbursement to be $500 for all Non-union personnel.
B1 Fund Revenue (Village of So. Blooming Grove Police)
o No significant changes.
o Police contract will be settled for 2015 thru 2016 and impact on the B Fund has
not been assessed as of this date.
B1 Fund Appropriations
o No significant changes.
o Police contract will be settled for 2015 thru 2016 and impact on the B1 Fund
has not been assessed as of this date.
o Added salary for 2 part-time police officers.
o Increased the glasses reimbursement to be $500 for all Non-union personnel.
DA Fund Revenue:
o No significant changes.

2 of 97

C:\Users\jmcdonald.NWDOMAIN\Dropbox (Town of New Windsor)\NW_Public_Sharing\Blooming Grove\2018 BG Adopted Budget 11142017.xlsx

2018 Town of Blooming Grove
Budget Comments
Budget Highlights
DA Fund Appropriations
o No significant changes.
DB Fund Appropriations
o Increased sales tax revenue by $12,000.
DB Fund Appropriations
o No significant changes.
SL01 Fund - Salisbury Lighting District
o Expenses are declining because we corrected the inventory in the district. We
also have approval for replacing the lights with LED and we expect this to
further reduce costs. The cost of these lights will be approximately $148, each
and it will be paid for from fund balance.
SL02 Fund - Oxford Lighting District
o Expenses are declining because we corrected the inventory in the district.
SM Fund - Ambulance Fund
o No significant changes.
SR01 Fund - Mt. Lodge Refuse District
o No significant changes. Current contract expires on December 31, 2019.
Funding included $10,000 in fund balance.
SR02 Fund - Townwide Refuse District
o The Townwide Refuse Contract was awarded to Interstate Waste Services and
the rate will be $15.34 in year one. The contract is renewable yearly for the next
four years (Year 2: $15.66, Year 3: $15.99, Year 4: $16.33, Year 5: $16.67.)
Total for year 1 will be $943,410.
Water and Sewer District Comments:
o Rates are sufficient for 2018. However, they will need to be reviewed in 2019.
SS02 Fund - Tappan Sewer District
o Consent Order to correct "Inflow and Inflitration" (I&I). If completed in one
project then the cost would be less since it would eliminate the set up close out
of any additional projects. Projected cost is currently esimated to be $800,000.
SS03 Fund - Camp LaGuardia Sewer District
o No significant changes.
SS04 Fund - Oxford Sewer District
o No significant changes.
SS05 Fund - Glenwood Sewer District
o No significant changes.
SS06 Fund - Bull Mill Sewer District
o The rates for this district are declining in 2018 because we are realigning
operating costs.

3 of 97

C:\Users\jmcdonald.NWDOMAIN\Dropbox (Town of New Windsor)\NW_Public_Sharing\Blooming Grove\2018 BG Adopted Budget 11142017.xlsx

2018 Town of Blooming Grove
Budget Comments
Budget Highlights
SW02 Fund - Oxford Water District
o No significant changes.
SW03 Fund - Tomahawk Water District
o This Water District will have the well house undergo significant renovations
in the 4th quarter of 2017 and it will be fully automated when completed.
SW04 Fund - Tappan Water District
o This Water District is in need of the following changes:
1. The wells are deep and the district is in need of a new well.
2. The water tank assessment completed in 2016 shows that the Tappan
water tower is in need of replacement. It has an open proposal providing
more information.
3. The Well house is in need of a chlorine shed to remove the chlorine
container from the well house for safety reasons. This will be included in
the 2018 plan.
4. There are about 3 to 4 more hydrants that need to be replaced.
SW05 Fund - Mt. View Water District
o One of the wells is leaking and as of September 2017 it has not been
stopped.
